{\footnotesize\ttfamily void kvm\+\_\+arch\+\_\+mmu\+\_\+enable\+\_\+log\+\_\+dirty\+\_\+pt\+\_\+masked (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structkvm}{kvm}} $\ast$}]{kvm,  }\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structkvm__memory__slot}{kvm\+\_\+memory\+\_\+slot}} $\ast$}]{slot,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{include_2linux_2kvm__types_8h_a6b24d771ac086ed549f3718479640d57}{gfn\+\_\+t}}}]{gfn\+\_\+offset,  }\item[{unsigned long}]{mask }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

kvm\+\_\+arch\+\_\+mmu\+\_\+enable\+\_\+log\+\_\+dirty\+\_\+pt\+\_\+masked -\/ enable dirty logging for selected PT level pages.

It calls kvm\+\_\+mmu\+\_\+write\+\_\+protect\+\_\+pt\+\_\+masked to write protect selected pages to enable dirty logging for them.

We need to care about huge page mappings\+: e.\+g. during dirty logging we may have such mappings. Here is the call graph for this function\+:
% FIG 121

kvm\+\_\+mmu\+\_\+clear\+\_\+dirty\+\_\+pt\+\_\+masked -\/ clear M\+MU D-\/bit for PT level pages, or write protect the page if the D-\/bit isn\textquotesingle{}t supported. @kvm\+: kvm instance @slot\+: slot to clear D-\/bit @gfn\+\_\+offset\+: start of the B\+I\+T\+S\+\_\+\+P\+E\+R\+\_\+\+L\+O\+NG pages we care about @mask\+: indicates which pages we should clear D-\/bit
